Base Cellio
-- WARNING: This schema is for context only and is not meant to be run.
-- Table order and constraints may not be valid for execution.

CREATE TABLE public.app_users (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
  username text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password text NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT app_users_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.cellars (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
  user_id uuid,
  name text NOT NULL,
  is_secondary boolean DEFAULT false,
  CONSTRAINT cellars_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT cellars_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES public.app_users(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.categories (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
  name text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  CONSTRAINT categories_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.product_templates (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
  category_id uuid,
  name text NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT product_templates_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT product_templates_category_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES public.categories(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.items (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
  user_id uuid,
  cellar_id uuid,
  categorie text NOT NULL,
  produit text NOT NULL,
  qte numeric DEFAULT 1,
  unite text DEFAULT 'Bouteille 75cl'::text,
  date_entree date DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
  date_peremption date,
  notes text,
  millesime text,
  format text,
  date_fabrication date,
  domaine text,
  CONSTRAINT items_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT items_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES public.app_users(id),
  CONSTRAINT items_cellar_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (cellar_id) REFERENCES public.cellars(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.user_settings (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  ntfy_topic text,
  CONSTRAINT user_settings_pkey PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.alert_rules (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid,
  categorie text,
  fridge_days integer,
  freezer_days integer,
  CONSTRAINT alert_rules_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

FROSTI (cogelo_app)
-- WARNING: This schema is for context only and is not meant to be run.
-- Table order and constraints may not be valid for execution.

CREATE TABLE public.app_users (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  username text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password text NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT app_users_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.freezers (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  user_id uuid,
  name text NOT NULL,
  is_fridge boolean DEFAULT false,
  CONSTRAINT freezers_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT freezers_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES public.app_users(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.categories (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  CONSTRAINT categories_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.product_templates (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  category_id uuid,
  name text NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT product_templates_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT product_templates_category_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES public.categories(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.items (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  user_id uuid,
  congelo_id uuid,
  categorie text NOT NULL,
  produit text NOT NULL,
  qte numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  unite text DEFAULT 'pièce(s)'::text,
  date_entree date DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
  date_peremption date,
  notes text,
  CONSTRAINT items_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT items_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES public.app_users(id),
  CONSTRAINT items_congelo_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (congelo_id) REFERENCES public.freezers(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.user_settings (
  user_id text NOT NULL,
  ntfy_topic text,
  CONSTRAINT user_settings_pkey PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.alert_rules (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  user_id text NOT NULL,
  categorie text NOT NULL,
  fridge_days integer DEFAULT 7,
  freezer_days integer DEFAULT 90,
  CONSTRAINT alert_rules_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

MEALIO
-- WARNING: This schema is for context only and is not meant to be run.
-- Table order and constraints may not be valid for execution.

CREATE TABLE public.official_ingredients (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  categorie text NOT NULL,
  nom text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  rayon text,
  default_storage text CHECK (default_storage = ANY (ARRAY['frosti'::text, 'cellio'::text])),
  default_is_fridge boolean DEFAULT true,
  CONSTRAINT official_ingredients_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.unit_mappings (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  unite text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  abreviation text,
  type_unite text,
  equivalence_reference text,
  notes text,
  multiplicateur numeric DEFAULT 1,
  CONSTRAINT unit_mappings_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.ignored_words (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  mot text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  type_ignore text DEFAULT 'parasite'::text,
  CONSTRAINT ignored_words_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.ingredient_synonyms (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  mot_recette text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  ingredient_id uuid NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT ingredient_synonyms_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT ingredient_synonyms_ingredient_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (ingredient_id) REFERENCES public.official_ingredients(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.ingredient_densities (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  ingredient_id uuid NOT NULL,
  unite text NOT NULL,
  poids_g_approx numeric NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT ingredient_densities_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT ingredient_densities_ingredient_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (ingredient_id) REFERENCES public.official_ingredients(id),
  CONSTRAINT ingredient_densities_unite_fkey FOREIGN KEY (unite) REFERENCES public.unit_mappings(unite)
);
CREATE TABLE public.ai_resolution_log (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  mot_recette text NOT NULL,
  proposition_ia text,
  ingredient_id_propose uuid,
  statut text DEFAULT 'en_attente'::text CHECK (statut = ANY (ARRAY['en_attente'::text, 'valide'::text, 'rejete'::text])),
  valide_par text,
  CONSTRAINT ai_resolution_log_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT ai_resolution_log_ingredient_id_propose_fkey FOREIGN KEY (ingredient_id_propose) REFERENCES public.official_ingredients(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.recurring_items (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id text NOT NULL,
  produit text NOT NULL,
  ingredient_id uuid,
  qte_minimum numeric NOT NULL,
  unite text NOT NULL,
  mode text DEFAULT 'auto'::text CHECK (mode = ANY (ARRAY['auto'::text, 'suggestion'::text])),
  CONSTRAINT recurring_items_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT recurring_items_ingredient_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (ingredient_id) REFERENCES public.official_ingredients(id),
  CONSTRAINT recurring_items_unite_fkey FOREIGN KEY (unite) REFERENCES public.unit_mappings(unite)
);
CREATE TABLE public.household_exclusions (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id text NOT NULL,
  ingredient_id uuid NOT NULL,
  type_exclusion text DEFAULT 'allergie'::text CHECK (type_exclusion = ANY (ARRAY['allergie'::text, 'regime'::text, 'aversion'::text])),
  notes text,
  CONSTRAINT household_exclusions_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT household_exclusions_ingredient_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (ingredient_id) REFERENCES public.official_ingredients(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.meal_plans (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id text NOT NULL,
  recipe_id uuid NOT NULL,
  scheduled_date date NOT NULL,
  meal_type text CHECK (meal_type = ANY (ARRAY['midi'::text, 'soir'::text])),
  role text DEFAULT 'plat'::text CHECK (role = ANY (ARRAY['entree'::text, 'plat'::text, 'accompagnement'::text, 'dessert'::text, 'autre'::text])),
  servings integer DEFAULT 4,
  CONSTRAINT meal_plans_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.meal_templates (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id text NOT NULL,
  nom text NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT meal_templates_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.meal_template_items (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  template_id uuid NOT NULL,
  recipe_id uuid NOT NULL,
  jour_semaine integer NOT NULL CHECK (jour_semaine >= 1 AND jour_semaine <= 7),
  meal_type text CHECK (meal_type = ANY (ARRAY['midi'::text, 'soir'::text])),
  role text DEFAULT 'plat'::text,
  servings integer DEFAULT 4,
  CONSTRAINT meal_template_items_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT meal_template_items_template_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (template_id) REFERENCES public.meal_templates(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.shopping_lists (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  user_id text NOT NULL,
  name text NOT NULL,
  status text DEFAULT 'en_cours'::text CHECK (status = ANY (ARRAY['en_cours'::text, 'terminee'::text])),
  period_start date,
  period_end date,
  CONSTRAINT shopping_lists_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.shopping_items (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  list_id uuid NOT NULL,
  produit text NOT NULL,
  ingredient_id uuid,
  qte numeric,
  unite text,
  is_checked boolean DEFAULT false,
  is_manual boolean DEFAULT false,
  ai_status text CHECK (ai_status = ANY (ARRAY['green'::text, 'orange'::text, 'red'::text, 'recurrent'::text])),
  CONSTRAINT shopping_items_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT shopping_items_list_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (list_id) REFERENCES public.shopping_lists(id),
  CONSTRAINT shopping_items_ingredient_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (ingredient_id) REFERENCES public.official_ingredients(id),
  CONSTRAINT shopping_items_unite_fkey FOREIGN KEY (unite) REFERENCES public.unit_mappings(unite)
);
CREATE TABLE public.shopping_item_recipes (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  shopping_item_id uuid NOT NULL,
  recipe_id uuid NOT NULL,
  recipe_nom text,
  qte_contribuee numeric,
  CONSTRAINT shopping_item_recipes_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT shopping_item_recipes_shopping_item_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (shopping_item_id) REFERENCES public.shopping_items(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.matcher_memory (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  ingredient_key text NOT NULL,
  stock_item_id text NOT NULL,
  source text NOT NULL CHECK (source = ANY (ARRAY['ai'::text, 'human'::text, 'text'::text, 'exact'::text])),
  confidence numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  validated boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  reason text,
  usage_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT matcher_memory_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.matcher_exclusions (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  ingredient_key text NOT NULL,
  stock_item_id text NOT NULL,
  reason text,
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT matcher_exclusions_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

COOKIWIKI

-- WARNING: This schema is for context only and is not meant to be run.
-- Table order and constraints may not be valid for execution.

CREATE TABLE public.recipes (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  title text NOT NULL,
  description text,
  image_url text,
  prep_time integer DEFAULT 0,
  cook_time integer DEFAULT 0,
  servings integer DEFAULT 4,
  difficulty text CHECK (difficulty = ANY (ARRAY['Facile'::text, 'Moyen'::text, 'Difficile'::text])),
  instructions text,
  ingredients jsonb DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  tags ARRAY DEFAULT '{}'::text[],
  last_modified_by uuid,
  updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  CONSTRAINT recipes_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT recipes_last_modified_by_fkey FOREIGN KEY (last_modified_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.comments (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  recipe_id uuid,
  user_id uuid,
  content text NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT comments_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT comments_recipe_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (recipe_id) REFERENCES public.recipes(id),
  CONSTRAINT comments_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.health_check (
  id integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (id = 1),
  last_ping timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT health_check_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

Placer au réfrigérateur pendant 12 à 15 heures (selon l'épaisseur de la viande).\\n4. Sortir le filet du sel, le rincer abondamment à l'eau claire pour retirer tout résidu de sel.\\n5. Placer dans le bac à légumes du réfrigérateur et laisser sécher pendant 2 à 3 semaines.\\n9.
